# M5.32 candidate ledger: the three Lagrangian searches, one table

> Rung R13-L of [M5.32](../tasks/m5_32_task_details.md). Compiled 2026-09-02 from three pinned records. Every row cites a script, a receipt, or a commit-pinned document; nothing in the status column is asserted from memory. The model author asked for exactly this (2026-09-02): which candidates from the three searches are the most promising, and what the repair options are for the two open issues.

Result vocabulary (adopted from the parallel program's 2026-08-31 review, used in every status cell): `ESTABLISHED_KINEMATIC` (a finite quantity on a chosen family, no dynamics), `ESTABLISHED_SYMMETRY` (an exact group action with a conserved charge), `MODE_EXISTS` (a localized linear mode on a converged background), `PERIODIC_ORBIT_EXISTS` (a nonlinear relative equilibrium), `CANDIDATE_REFUTED` (a pre-registered gate failed on that candidate), `NUMERICALLY_UNRESOLVED` (the instrument could not decide). A refutation refutes the candidate as run, never the question.

## 1. The problem, stated once

### 1.1 The field and the certified 3+1 action (OpenWave, the model author's M5 with the 4x4 promotion)

```text
M(x)          real symmetric 4x4, vacuum M_vac = diag(-sg, 1, delta, 0) = diag(+g, 1, delta, 0) on the code branch s = -1 (N = M eta spectrum (-g, 1, delta, 0); sign line corrected by R20-0, 2026-09-13), g = 8 or 32, delta = 0.3
A_mu          = d_mu M                                    (the jets)
F_mu nu       = A_mu eta A_nu - A_nu eta A_mu             (curvature, quadratic in the jets)
<F, G>_eta    = tr(eta F eta G^T)
I1            = sum_{mu<nu} eta^mumu eta^nunu <F_mu nu, F_mu nu>_eta = (1/2) F_abcd F^abcd
V4            = w sum_{p=1..4} (tr((M eta)^p) - C_p)^2,   C_p = (s g)^p + 1 + delta^p,  w = 7.24023879e-4
L_cert        = -4 I1 - V4                                (CERTIFIED_COEFFS)
```

### 1.2 The two open issues, as gates

```text
NEWTON SIGN     two defects at separation d: E_int(d) = E(pair) - 2 E(single).  Physics wants dE_int/dd > 0
                (attraction).  Under L_cert the boost-dressed pair is REPULSIVE on every instrument run (R0, R3, R11).
OMEGA DIVERGENCE  the clock is the isorotation M(t) = R(omega t) M R(omega t)^T in the (2,3) eigenplane, tangent a0.
                Fixed charge J = 2 kin omega, energy E_J = E_stat + J^2/(4 kin).  With the RIGID isorotation the
                inertia kin = -4 x (omega^2 coefficient of I1) grows linearly with the box (kin ~ kappa L, R4, R7, R10,
                R12), so omega* = J/(2 kin) ~ 1/L: the frequency is a box label, not a property of the object.
```

The parallel program's 2026-08-31 group fact sharpens the second issue: the continuous stabilizer of `diag(-g, 1, delta, 0)` in SO(1,3) is trivial (the discrete part is the Klein four-group, the OpenWave R9 receipt), so NO exact S^1 fixes the certified vacuum. Any clock on this vacuum is either rigid (extensive inertia) or weighted by hand (kinematic only, `w -> c w` rescales `omega*` freely). A localized clock therefore needs one of: a degenerate eigenvalue pair somewhere (a vacuum with `d2 = d3`, or a wall where it holds), an added field whose orbit norm vanishes in the vacuum, or a different action.

**Net OpenWave position.** No constant-coefficient term at current order fixes either issue on the certified vacuum. The one obstruction every clock rung ran into is the rigid isorotation of the vacuum frame far from the defect, which is a property of the clock convention (Q60), not of the object or of any term. The Newton sign has never been reversed on this action by any local mechanism.

## 3. Vantasner substrate-framework, P239 to P249

Different action from § 2 in every row: the P239/P240 base is `L = -1/2 F F eta eta h h - V_M5.17` with the internal metric `h = eta - 2 P_t eta` (`P_t` the simple timelike spectral projector of `eta^-1 M`), plus a projector current, a Landau-de Gennes lift with exterior spectrum `(g, 1, 0, 0)`, and later an auxiliary clock axis with locks. So its exterior is ALREADY degenerate in the tangent pair (two zero eigenvalues), unlike our `(-g, 1, delta, 0)`. P249 is a third action (Sym(3) + complex scalar + locks). Records split across `proposals/` and `campaigns/`; 30 accepted claims in this range. Digest with verbatim quotes and the claim boundaries C-M5C-001 to 004: `scratchpad/r13l/vantasner_digest.md` (local, not tracked). None of the P239 to P248 numbers was re-derived by us in this rung (§ 7).

**Net Vantasner position.** "no campaign has an accepted attractive Newton tail between separately relaxed M5 defects": the attractive statements are an added dilaton's linearized exchange (P239 H), a conditional on an unmeasured cross inertia (P240 D), an imported induced coupling out of regime (P243), or a frozen telescope (P247). The clock line ends in P249, on a new action. No campaign uses `omega(x)`, walls, or frequency interfaces (one grep hit, an acoustic interface in a paper review); the repo's stated rule is only against IMPOSED support (taper, mask, pinned wall, cutoff, box) as a localization device. Vocabulary note: the tokens `ESTABLISHED_KINEMATIC`, `PERIODIC_ORBIT_EXISTS`, `CANDIDATE_REFUTED` come from the 2026-08-31 review comment and do not occur in the repo at the pin; "rung packet" is not a repo term. The repo's unit is an obligation node (`requires`, `pass_licenses`, `does_not_license`, `maximum_verdict`, `failure_scope`, `unlocks`) with a typed license chain (object, symmetry or conservation, ensemble, variational functional, admissible space, representation coverage, observable, numerical representation, permitted verdict) and, since PR #192, an analytic-closure receipt before any production numerics.

## 4. Mikulski the-final-lagrangian-of-physics, reports 001 to 013

Same field, same `F`, same vacuum signature (`diag(-g, 1, delta, 0)`, g = 8), so these rows are directly comparable to § 2. Convention: the reports' `I_1 = F_{mnab} F^{mnab}` is exactly twice our `I1` (audit script D.4), so their `-I_1 = -4 omega^2` on the clock witness reads `-4 I1 = -8 omega^2` in our units; every sign statement survives. Their "3x3-preserving family" means the static 3x3 sector (time row of `M` constant). Stack: torch autograd, 32^3 box at h = 1.5 (L = 48), pinned vacuum shell, the working metric `G = eta + 2 u u` on the matrix slots (our `h` of the C2 family). Reproduced here: 001 (2 s, every quoted number matches), 007 (the self-contained legs and the numpy route 2 on the committed fields, 1.8e-15; the lattice producers need report 004's GPU fields and did not run here); 006 (744 s CPU, both routes pass, every README number matches: chain-of-7 ratio 1.8037, IR slope 85.35, `X = +11.630`, gaussian pocket -0.8204; the `I4/I1 = 0.763` lattice cost is an external artifact needing the 004 fields). Digest with the verbatim headline sentences: `scratchpad/r13l/mikulski_digest.md` (local, not tracked).

**Net Mikulski position (the author's own words, 013):** "the energy-functional reading remains the only reading with a convergence-certified localized clock". No box ladder had been run for a boost clock at the `65c6177f` pin; it was run on our request of 2026-09-02 (the [appendix L-ladder](https://github.com/mjmikulski/the-final-lagrangian-of-physics/blob/20cfb025a5e86cde167e69e0be45ff50f4a01117/reports/008-i1-squared-clock/APPENDIX-L-ladder.md), 2026-09-14, read at `20cfb025`: L 48 / 72 / 96 at h 1.5, gamma fixed at 70.61); the Newton line is a chain of closures identical in content to ours; no wall, interface, or `omega(x)` construct exists in the repo (grep over all markdown at the pin). One structural fact stated there and true of our stack too: `F_0i = [dM/dt, d_i M]_eta` vanishes wherever `d_i M = 0`, so the VACUUM carries no kinetic energy under any clock rotation; the extensive inertia is the defect's orientation tail (`1/r`) times a non-decaying generator, on both stacks.

**Outcome (2026-09-02, the rung ran the same day; [task record](../tasks/m5_32_task_details.md), audit in `m5_32_r13w_audit.py`).** W0 closure 22/22 with four theorems the packet had not anticipated: every planar profile has `E_u = 0` (walls are tensionless, `sigma_0 = h V4`), the phase field on the uniform vacuum has no action at all, a planar orientation twist in a non-commuting plane carries inertia at zero static cost (so the fixed-J functional has no minimizer on `L_cert`), and the shell sharpens to the lattice floor. W1 FAIL as pre-registered (`sigma_0 ~ h^1.000 -> 0`, the released layer unstable). W2 PASS on the frozen gates, both of which turned out to be identities of any planar slab (unfalsifiable on that geometry, no evidential weight). W3 FAIL: twenty fixed-J relaxations, none stationary, the shell melting, the inertia of its inner flank inflating without bound in an eigenvalue zigzag (the W0 S2 density at lattice scale), `E_J` rising with the shell radius, `omega` falling with iterations and as `h^1.7`, box-independent. Verdict: the wall convention on the certified action is `ESTABLISHED_KINEMATIC` at best; the omega issue is reframed as the absence of a phase-gradient cost in `-4 I1`.

Owed OpenWave items unchanged and NOT in this rung: the 12000-iteration ring ladder, the relaxed-ring fixed J with a box-scaled taper, the `(F.F)^2` dilation test against a flipped static sector, the term catalog, the g = 32 relaxation (PAUSE RECORD 3). One item from § 5.2 is cheap and decisive for a rank-3 candidate and belongs to its author's stack, not ours: the L-ladder of the Mikulski 008 well.

### 6.3 The next OpenWave rung ladder: R14, the two-derivative class and the coexistence conjecture (a one-day autonomous run)

Posted before any number is produced. The inputs that reorder the ranking after R13-W, both received on the coordination thread: the model author's 2026-09-03 analysis (with an AI assistant; treated as evidence, not resolution, until R14-0 confirms it with our own scripts) and the parallel program's P250 (verified by our [`m5_32_p250_check.py`](../scripts/m5_32_p250_check.py), 24/24). Three facts move the plan: (1) P249's whole radiation edge comes from the explicit axis lock, and on the 4x4 degenerate vacuum four of the five Goldstones are clock-charged, so a strict fixed-J minimizer is excluded in any orientation-invariant M5 (rank 1's field map would inherit massless charged channels); (2) the two-derivative terms `K_lambda` (eigenvalue-only kinetic), `R_G` (the mixed-trace, Einstein-Hilbert-like contraction, a total derivative on every fixed-eigenvalue Lorentz orbit) and `K_P` (the projector kinetic term with phase stiffness `(lambda_2 - lambda_3)^2 |d phi|^2` on the (2,3) block) are LOWER order than R1's quartic basis and were never closed on any stack; `K_P` is exactly the phase-gradient cost R13-W found missing in `-4 I1`, and it is the inertia structure `iota = f^2 + 4 b^2` that P250's bag runs on; (3) the author's conjecture: within local second-order covariant Lagrangians a strict fixed-J minimizer clock and a Coulomb hedgehog cannot coexist, because the R13-W sheet and the hedgehog tail live in the same tilt channel and every two-derivative term that charges one diverges on the other. R14 tests the conjecture as a linear program over a frozen term basis, and runs the decisive numeric test on the named candidate regardless of the LP's answer. Scripts the author mentioned as attached did not reach the thread (Discussions cannot carry `.py`); when posted they become the audit baseline, R14-0 builds ours independently either way.

**Two things stated before the go.** First, the author's packet stage 0 asks for a "canonical-action audit" of signs and generator normalization: on our stack every term is carried in E-orientation (static `+`, inertia `+ omega^2`), which IS the author's `-c K` Lagrangian convention, so the sign correction changes nothing in our numbers and stage 0 reduces to the symmetry-defect gate and the spectral-domain statement (folded into R16-0 and the instrument gates). **Outcome of R16-0 (2026-09-06, the verification rung ran 12:42 to 13:38 UTC; the [task record](../tasks/m5_32_task_details.md) R16-0 section for every number and the audit).** Audited 13 / 4 / 0 (17 claims). C1: the counterexample exact; on the witness jet the two completions coincide, on the witness lattice they differ by 24 to 46 percent (not 2 to 6), and the author's posted h column is `I_rebuild / 4` while ours is `I_norm` (the R15 3.0-vs-4 mismatch closed: the completion, not the pair ordering). C2: Coleman's diagnosis reproduced with the numbers (the sextic crossing at 0.0090, `s* = 0.2236`); two normalizations named (`omega_c^2 = mu / (4 c_P)` for `U = mu rho^2`; the author's 0.01117 is the value at `s*`, the minimum is `mu`); the sextic's Q-ball needs J above 2.6e4 and a radius above 69, outside every lattice box of the record. C3: `E_h >= 0` pointwise for both completions (our floor sentence retracted); `I_rebuild` keeps the sign flip on the relaxed hedgehog (`+195 / +629 / +1500`), `I_norm` does not. C4: the local circle is exact on the potential and `K_P^23` (jets exact, lattice `O(h^2)`), not on `I1`, not on either completion, and NOT on the regulator `E2` either: `L_v4` as written is not circle-invariant, the average must cover `E2` (the R16 instrument will). C5, C6: every sheet formula exact (the boost-sheet law reads `(g + delta +- s)^2`). C7: the P-iv end state has an oblate uniaxial center (the R15 crossing) and a `beta^2 = 1` ring of radius 2.9 with the great-circle quadrupole signature, axis on the lattice body diagonal, identical in cell units on both boxes: the Landau-de Gennes topology at lattice scale, refinement owed. C8: `J_z = 0` on axisymmetric configurations confirmed (`O(h^2)`), the `2Y_2m` diagnostic built and gated, the P-iv split achiral. C9: the pattern 0 / small / large / 0 reproduced. Net: object v4 survives R16-0 with one definitional repair (the averaged regulator) and two normalization notes; the second go builds the instrument.

**Outcome of R16 (2026-09-06 13:54 to 2026-09-07 01:45 UTC, the instrument and the four stages; the [task record](../tasks/m5_32_task_details.md) R16 section, the [method note § 14](m5_32_method_note.md), one independent auditor per stage).** The instrument realized v4 under the two readings with every term circle-averaged (43 gates; 8 samples exact on the lattice, where the density has degree 4 in alpha, 4 in the descents). R16-1: `UNIAXIAL_RADIAL` on n32 L48, n48 L72 and n64 L48 (h 0.75), the seed's split shrinking tenfold, the exterior `r^-4`, the radial hedgehog at Morse index 0 in the split sector (audit 4/3/0). Net: v4 at `(1e-2, 1, 0.4)` has no bound doublet on the relaxed core and no relative equilibrium at fixed K on the lattice, and the statics go uniaxial; the next instrument fix is the true fixed-K gradient; the results post (the TL;DR shape, the method-note body with three plots inline) went up on the coordination thread on 2026-09-07 01:40 UTC ([discussioncomment-18323589](https://github.com/vantasnerdan/substrate-framework/discussions/186#discussioncomment-18323589)).

### 6.6 The next OpenWave rung ladder: R17, the true fixed-K gradient, the dual-reading falsifier, and the author's v6 and c_X candidates (PROPOSED 2026-09-08, revised the same day against the rev-100 report; RAN 2026-09-08, the outcome at the end of this section)

The inputs that set this ladder ([`m5_32_convo.md`](../tasks/m5_32_convo.md), the 2026-09-07 and 09-08 entries; every number evidence, not resolution, until our scripts reproduce it): the author's 2026-09-07 reply to R16 (object v5, four requests), the 2026-09-08 status update (the Astra-consolidated report at revisions 44 to 85: the dual reading, the falsifier, the `c_X X_M^2` clock candidate, eight requests), and, read the same day, the report itself at revision 100 (185 pages, [`theory/duda_2026-09-08_m5_final_lagrangian_approaches_v3.pdf`](../../theory/duda_2026-09-08_m5_final_lagrangian_approaches_v3.pdf), zenodo 22654888, gitignored), with the 53-page soliton note and the 233-page illustrated Complete Picture Report 019 beside it (same folder, zenodo 22370750 and 22656059). The rev-100 report supersedes both thread comments on four points, each adopted below: (1) v5 is withdrawn by the author's own § 26 (a positive-definite `V(rho)` on a degenerate-pair vacuum has the static minimizer `rho = 0`, so R16-1's uniaxial core "is what any such potential gives"; the sextic governs the rotating branch only) and its `(nu, kappa)` violates the local collision gate of § 27.3 (`nu / kappa < 2 gap^2 = 0.029` against v5's 0.10); the object is now v6 with a core coupling. (2) Request (iv)'s attractive-tail prediction is withdrawn in § 25.3 ("the wrong object"); the replacement request is the doublet operator's lowest mode decomposed by shell angular index, separating the `+2 / r^2` connection floor from the potential's well, and whether a binding region is the unwound core. (3) Request (i) was partly done by the author's second agent from our published central-line spectra (§ 27.1: `min d^2 V4 / d s^2 = -0.0081` at h 1.5 and `-0.0106` at h 0.75, weakly attractive; the bound doublet killed by the angular barrier alone, Bargmann count below 0.011), so our stage reads the full fields and checks that reconstruction. (4) Request 2 now has its definitions (§ 28: `K_coll = 2 omega Delta_min^2 int f^2 d^3x`, `f` the peak-normalized profile of the doublet mode, `Delta_min = min_core (a - b)` the director-pair gap, the requirement `r_0 sqrt(mu) >= 0.5` with R16's core read at 0.30), so it moves from "not computed" into R17-0. Two repair proposals in 24 hours (v6 from the author's line, `c_X` from the second agent's) on one instrument; our own queued fix (the true fixed-K gradient, the R16-3 audit's finding) runs first because it re-decides our own record before any new object is judged on it. The Complete Picture report's § 7.5.2 inspected our R16 code and JSON and states that the tensor end fields (local, gitignored by policy) were not obtainable; whether to publish the n32 fields is the user's call, listed at the go.

(2) The report's § 79 constraint (every prefactor built from scale-invariant ratios, or the equivalence principle fails by twelve orders; `c_s rho^2 E2` with `rho` an eigenvalue difference "fails", the core coupling "must be checked") is a v9-sequence result about gravity, parked in § 7; it does not change what R17 runs but it is the first place the author's own line constrains v6's form. (3) The Faber, Kleinert-Zaanen and teleparallel identifications (§§ 48 to 50, 64, 69 to 81) are reading claims, parked in § 7; the "M5's degeneracy is the f(T) strong-coupling problem" sentence is the one worth a later reading task. (4) The report lists twelve overturned pre-registered predictions on its own side; ours are counted the same way in the task record. (5) DECIDED 2026-09-08: the R16 end fields are handed over as a data release on the OpenWave repository (tag [`m5_32-r16-fields`](https://github.com/openwave-labs/openwave/releases/tag/m5_32-r16-fields), created by the user 2026-09-08; the 12 arrays with `README.md`, `RELEASE_NOTES.md` and `SHA256SUMS.txt`, staged locally in `checkpoints/m5_32_r16/handover/`, 122 MB), not committed to the tree (the dataset policy stands); the results post links the release. (6) The results post goes up on #186 AFTER the run, in the method-note shape, and asks for the script bundle's location (not attached to zenodo 22654888).

**Outcome of R17 (2026-09-08 13:53 UTC go, the run's [task record](../tasks/m5_32_task_details.md) R17 section, the [method note § 15](m5_32_method_note.md), one auditor per stage).** R17-0 (audited 7 / 1 / 1): the falsifier is decided against the report's number, not against the dual reading: the tail `8 (1 - delta)^4` is our `E_h` unit exactly, and the pair coefficient `4 (1 - delta)^4 / (pi d)` is the superposition cross term `8 pi A / d = 48.27 / d` divided by `16 pi^2` (a Heaviside-Lorentz potential attached to a Gaussian field; the ratio pair / tail is convention-free, `8 pi`), `NORMALIZATION_SUPERPOSITION`; the record's like-charge pair rises with d (a string), `PAIR_LAW_NOT_CERTIFIED`, so the comparison the report asks for has no measured number on either stack. The second agent's reconstruction (`-0.0081 / -0.0106`, `Delta_min 0.0525 / 0.1203`, `r_0 sqrt(mu) 0.30`) reproduces to four digits on the central lines (not grid-converged). `X_M^2 = -2 I1 - I2 + 4 I3` exactly (`IN_SPAN`), `X_M = d_mu J^mu` (`TOTAL_DERIVATIVE`), `l = dX_M / dA_0` zero on the curl-free hedgehog and a lattice residual on static cores. The two agents' symbol disagreement: the Complete Picture report is right (a radial characteristic with the exact `{0,0,1,1,1,1,2,2,2,6}` spectrum on the hedgehog axis) and structurally so (`H_00` positive semidefinite: the hunt report's (3, 2, 5) is impossible); transverse characteristics too. The instrument (21 gates; the true a0 chain rule at complex-step precision): the bare static core is a `1 / split^2` singularity of `E_K` under the true gradient, a protocol fact the frozen descents could not see. R17-1: n32 K 50 a stationary delocalized state 27 percent above the delocalized bound (the R16-3 spike replaced by the box's split), K 200 escape (d) on a higher branch, n64 descending and unresolved. R17-2: the director-relative weight is `P23` exactly, the core melts further under it, and the doublet spectrum does not move (0.0447 vs 0.0448, the same box mode; the empty box 0.0256 under both weights): § 24.3 refuted, the mechanism a third of a `K_P` barrier that is mostly the projector's own; the lowest mode is pure l = 2 on a box shell, no binding region. R17-3: the split-free core is the saddle of `U_v6` at every `g_W`; a core-seeded split decays at 0.5, 1.1 and 1.35 and holds at 2.0 while the core melts past the instrument's domain (escape d), the condensation threshold bracketing in (1.35, 2.0] above the author's 0.53 / 1.12 / 1.35 (the energies unconverged, the audit's qualification); the doublet operator on v6: the control's converged read at 2.0 is pulled inward but not bound (0.0349 vs the box's 0.0256), the seeded 0.5 and 1.1 fields' operators converged to the box mode (0.0451, 0.0445), the 1.35 solve did not converge and the bounded read finds no trial below the box, the well turning negative inside the core while `K_P`'s stiffening keeps the doublet unbound and the connection term persists inside the core (against (2') and (3')); fixed K on the 1.35 field refuted at K 50 and 200 by escape (d). R17-4: on the static radial core the `c_X` inertia is the lattice residual of `l` (1.6e-6 `c_X`), no crossing at `c_X <= 100`, the crossing extrapolating to 2e5. Deviations (five, in the task record): the nucleated-shell seeds, the core-seeded v6 statics, the 12-P-core run shape, the doublet frame's handedness, the symbol script's mirroring; the fixed-K reads' lift dependence found by the R17-2 audit and fixed for the record. What closes: the falsifier as posed (its number, not the reading), § 24.3, prediction (1') at `g_W <= 1.35` and (4') at K 50 / 200 on this box, the `c_X` route on static cores. What stays open: a like-charge pair on the degenerate vacuum (the comparison the corrected falsifier needs), v6 above `g_W` 1.35 outside the instrument's admissible domain (the § 30.1 conflict is now a lattice fact), the n64 true-gradient branch, the `X_M` term on a rotating core. Reported to the author on #186 after the run in the method-note shape, with the definition asks (which object carries `c_X`; "relative to the director's eigenvalue" as we read it; the `W` used) and the request for the script bundle's location.

### 6.7 The next OpenWave rung ladder: R18, the radial solve for `r_0 sqrt(mu)`, the condensate and its Goldstone reads, and the like-charge pair on the degenerate vacuum (PROPOSED 2026-09-09; RAN 2026-09-09, the outcome at the end of this section)

The inputs that set this ladder ([`m5_32_convo.md`](../tasks/m5_32_convo.md), the 2026-09-09 entries; every number evidence, not resolution, until our scripts reproduce it): the author's reply to R17 (2026-09-09 06:39 UTC, "both corrections accepted, the falsifier is dead, and the clock may be the wrong mode", written against report revision 155) and the same day's status post at revision 200 (the report reordered by evidential tier, [`theory/duda_2026-09-09_m5_final_lagrangian_approaches_rev200.pdf`](../../theory/duda_2026-09-09_m5_final_lagrangian_approaches_rev200.pdf), zenodo 22675440, 377 pages, gitignored). ### 6.8 The next OpenWave rung ladder: R19, the mixed bracket (the anticommutator in the time / gravity sector) at the form level, on the static Newton instruments, and on the clock (PROPOSED 2026-09-10, HELD; REVISED 2026-09-11 with the reading answered by the author's rev-294 report; RUN 2026-09-11/12, the OUTCOME at the end of the section)

The inputs that set this ladder ([`m5_32_convo.md`](../tasks/m5_32_convo.md), the 2026-09-10 entries; every claim evidence, not resolution, until our scripts reproduce it): the author's 2026-09-10 03:18 UTC comment on Discussion #186 ([discussioncomment-18379326](https://github.com/vantasnerdan/substrate-framework/discussions/186#discussioncomment-18379326)) and the same text to the list at 03:22 UTC, from a chat with Opus: "commutator for the spatial / EM sector, anticommutator for the time / gravity sector", the commutator's antisymmetric content read as odd spin (like charges repel) and the anticommutator's trace plus traceless-symmetric content as spin 0 plus spin 2 (attractive), the Savvidy tensor gauge fields named as the literature match, "please try, I will also do"; and the author's 07:19 UTC list message to Faber listing three repair ideas for the Newton sign (a different time treatment, imaginary or with the commutator changed in time; Mikulski's imaginary mass, "how to realize it?"; the field's near-constant electron-like frequency). The proposal does not say which of three readings of "the time / gravity sector" is meant, and the three differ in what they can change; the pre-registration check below decides that before any run.

**What the pre-registration check settles before the go** ([`m5_32_r19_precheck.py`](../scripts/m5_32_r19_precheck.py), [`m5_32_r19_precheck.json`](../data/m5_32_r19_precheck.json), 10 / 10 lines that can fail, 400 random jets; algebra only, no relaxation). Audited the same day by a fresh agent with its own loops ([`m5_32_r19_precheck_audit.py`](../scripts/m5_32_r19_precheck_audit.py), [`m5_32_r19_precheck_audit.json`](../data/m5_32_r19_precheck_audit.json), 600 jets plus uniform, time-row-rescaled and non-static sets): 8 / 8 CONFIRMED, C7 QUALIFIED, the sharpenings folded into the rows below. Notation: `P_mu nu = A_mu eta A_nu`, `F = P - P^T` (the certified commutator), `G = P + P^T` (the anticommutator), the densities with the FULL derivative contraction `(1/2) sum_{mu nu} eta^mu eta^nu <X, X>_eta` (the `sum_{mu<nu}` form is a complete Lorentz contraction only for objects antisymmetric in the derivative pair, which `G` is not: on `G` it is not boost invariant).

| C2 the identity | entrywise `G^2 + F^2 = 2 (P^2 + P^T2)`, so `GG + I1 = 4 PP` exactly (1.9e-16): the anticommutator square is the certified action plus four times the product norm; the audit adds that the `sum_{mu<nu}` half-sum of `G` (the notebook form of `I1`) is not even rotation invariant (it drops `G_11`, `G_22`, `G_33`) and is exactly quadratic in `omega`: the `omega^4` of C8 comes entirely from the diagonal `G_00` block the full contraction adds |

Two readings the check cannot settle and one remark carried to the author: which of (T), (B) fixed-frame, (B) with `u`, or the pure anticommutator (A) is meant, and whether the `(0, 0)` corner (`G_00 = 2 A_0 eta A_0`, which the commutator cannot produce) belongs to the gravity sector; and the mediator reading itself: every term here is quartic in the jets, so it has NO quadratic fluctuation about the constant vacuum (the author's own § 33.1, the silent exterior), and the static interaction the record measures is between textures at nonlinear order (the boost dressing, R3), not a linear exchange whose spin fixes a sign. The spin statement is exact for the internal content (`F eta` is in `so(1, 3)`, the antisymmetric spin-1 content; `G` carries the trace and the traceless-symmetric parts; a jet proportional to `eta` drops out of `F` and not of `G`, while traceless-symmetric jets DO enter `F`); whether that content sets the sign of a texture-texture force is what R19-1 measures. The audit adds the representation-theory fact behind C1: the space of Lorentz-equivariant bilinears in `(A_mu, A_nu)` antisymmetric in the derivative pair and symmetric in the internal pair is ONE-dimensional, spanned by `H = tr(A_mu eta) A_nu - tr(A_nu eta) A_mu`, which vanishes identically on `eta`-traceless jets (the only part `F` sees); so a Savvidy-type field strength on this field needs an epsilon, a fixed vector, or the field's `u`.

**The object, once.** The paper's mechanism as the report reads it (§§ 363 to 364): the spatial vacuum `(1, delta, 0)` has three eigenvectors; a hedgehog winds one of them radially and the other two form the transverse pair, whose splitting is `delta` (winding on the 1-axis, the record's electron), `1` (the `delta`-axis) or `1 - delta` (the 0-axis); the three energies are the three lepton masses, scored by Koide `Q = sum m / (sum sqrt m)^2 = 2/3` (the observed ratios 1 : 206.77 : 3477.2). On the lattice the record's seed is `M3 = n n^T + delta phi phi^T` with `n` radial and `phi` azimuthal (`m5_21_4_a_pair._tensor_from_nhat`), isotropic-blended at the core (`r_c = 4`), embedded with `M_00 = -g`. The three-axis seeds assign the eigenvalues `(lambda_r, lambda_phi, lambda_theta)` to `(r, phi, theta)`:

```text
S_1     (1, delta, 0)       the record's electron (the transverse splitting delta)
S_d     (delta, 1, 0)       the delta-axis object (the transverse splitting 1)
S_0     (0, 1, delta)       the 0-axis object (the transverse splitting 1 - delta)
S_k'    the swapped transverse assignment of each (the control on the smallest box)
```

Two facts pre-registered against the report's reading. (i) Topology: the biaxial order-parameter space `SO(3) / D_2` has `pi_2 = 0` (R10; the degree the record reads is the leading eigenvector's `RP^2` degree, not an invariant), so on a vacuum with all four eigenvalues distinct NOTHING protects any of the three windings; the report's § 363.6 item 3 ("biaxiality is essential") is the opposite requirement from the paper's own protection, which lives on the uniaxial `(1, delta, delta)` where the transverse pair is degenerate. Escape is a live outcome. (ii) The transverse pair has a line defect on the polar axis in every construction (`phi` and `theta` are undefined there, the pair must degenerate or wind), which is the split core of R16 to R18 and the string of R18-3 seen from the single object; its cost per unit length is the tension measured there (0.8 to 1.2), so an energy linear in the box is a live outcome too. Neither outcome is a failure of the run; each is a measured answer to § 363.

**R20-0, the form level (one script, `m5_32_r20_0_class.py`, audited).** (a) The full `sym4` Hessians of `V4` and `V_spec` at the vacuum (10 x 10; the six conjugation directions zero, the four spectral curvatures as in the table). (b) `V_spec`'s exact lattice gradient (`d tr[P(Q)^2] = 2 tr[P(Q) P'(Q) dQ]`, `Q = M eta`, symmetrized) FD-gated at the R19 entrants' bar (1e-12 relative on a 4-point stencil). (c) `gamma` fixed by matching the potential energy of the record's relaxed single under `V_spec` to its `V4` energy (one number, reported). (d) The flat-vacuum quadratic form of `L_cert` on the 30-jet space (the R19 certificate's channels): zero on every jet, stated once for the TT question. (e) The measured pair laws on record, tabulated against the README's linear-potential statement (no run).

**R20-1, the three-axis solve on the certified static sector `4 I1 + V4` (g 8, delta 0.3, the paper's split vacuum).** The R3 / R19 instrument (`m5_32_r19_1_pair.descend`: FIRE, the pin shell of depth 1.6 Dirichlet at the seed, the kill rules RUNAWAY / DIVERGED / LOCUS-HIT), the static fast path (9 derivative pairs). Budget 4500 accepted steps (three times R3's, the R19 lesson), the convergence gate pre-registered: CONVERGED iff the relative energy drift over the last third of the accepted steps is below 1e-3 AND `max |G|` fell two decades below the seed's; else FALLING and the budget doubled once (the R19 extension stage), the label carried into every quoted number. The ladder n32 L48, n48 L72, n64 L96 at h 1.5. Reads per object and box: `E_k(L)`, the degree of EACH eigenvector on the shells r 6 / 9 / 12 (the escape read), the eigenvalue gaps and the biaxiality on the polar axis and in the core ball (the string read), the `E(L)` slope. Controls on n32 L48: the three swapped assignments `S_k'`, the degenerate vacuum `(g, 1, delta, delta)` for `S_d` and `S_0` (they must coincide there: the mechanism's own null), and `S_1` at g 32 (the instrument's other vacuum; R3's record 18.970 at n32, 21.401 at n48). Outcomes, per axis and then for the triple:

```text
AXIS_ESCAPES          the winding unwinds (the eigenvector's degree on the outer shells drops to 0)
AXIS_STRING           E_k grows linearly with L (the polar line defect leaves the box)
AXIS_CONVERGED        E_k(L) saturates on the ladder at the CONVERGED label
THREE_AXES_DISTINCT   three converged energies, ordered, the ratios and Koide Q reported
AXES_DEGENERATE       converged energies equal within the ladder's resolution
```

The Koide read on static energies (`Q` against 2/3, the ratios against 1 : 206.77 : 3477.2) is a read, not a gate: the report's own § 364 shows `Q = 2/3` is reachable at ratios 1 : 4.5 : 162, and the report's masses include a clock energy this field does not have (R13-W).

**R20-2, the same three axes on `4 I1 + V_spec` (the author's "first thing to test", § 369 item 1).** `gamma` from R20-0 (c), n32 L48 and n48 L72, the same gate, reads and outcomes; the comparison row `V_spec` against `V4` per axis.

**Sizing.** R19's undressed single took 1839 s for 1500 accepted steps on n32 and 7614 s on n48; at 4500 steps the n32 rows take about 1.5 h, the n48 rows about 6 h, the n64 rows (2.4 times the cells) about 15 h: 9 main jobs plus 7 controls plus 6 `V_spec` jobs on 12 workers, one overnight, the n64 rows the long pole; the n32 and n48 results are in hand within 8 h of the go.

**Not in R20, and why.** The `l = 2` shell (a radial-BVP object of the report, § 337); the Lorentz / Magnus force (dynamics, no integrator on this stack); the photon with transverse structure (§ 369, a page of algebra on the report's side); rung 2's vortex loop (a closed disclination loop of the transverse pair with its `E(R)`: an R21 candidate on this stack, the R14 ring precedent); the baryons (the author's next focus, "continuing started in OpenWave": M5.22-series scope, not M5.32); `(inc M)^2`, G-02 and the bounce stay parked (§ 6.8); the README's open calculations 1 to 6 wait for the bundle. The audits: a fresh agent per stage with its own scripts, the record in the task record and in § 8.

### 6.10 R20 RUN 2026-09-13 (09:50 UTC go, the results collected 00:00 UTC 09-14): the three-axis hedgehog on the certified static sector and on `V_spec`, with the Derrick, frame and radial reads the day's comments asked for

Read together, in the pre-registered vocabulary, after the audit (50 own lines, 41 PASS, the 9 FAILs refuting readings, folded in): (i) **`AXIS_STRING` is the label the two-rung rule returns for all three, and it is supported for `S_d` only**: its increment (+20.1 at equal budget, +22.9 against the extended n32 row) equals the tube prediction and is 3.4 to 3.8 times the n64 drift, and the growth is interior (15.3 to 38.1 with the frozen shell fixed); `S_1`'s increment (+1.8 / +3.3) and `S_0`'s (+0.5 / +8.2) are inside what the descents themselves move in an extension (the n32 rows dropped 1.5 and 7.6 in theirs), so those two labels are withdrawn; the flat middle of the polar tube gives a tension of 0.12 per unit length for `S_d` (a quarter of its increment inside rho 3, the rest of the line defect outside), 0.06 for `S_0`, 0.01 for `S_1`. (ii) **The pinned shell holds the seed's line defect for the `delta`-axis and `0`-axis objects.** 72 percent of `S_d`'s energy (45.5 of 63.6, the same 45.7 at n64) and 36 percent of `S_0`'s (11.0 of 30.4) sit in the Dirichlet layer where the seed's azimuthal and polar textures cross the polar axis and cannot relax; `S_1` keeps 7 percent there; the tube tension quoted first (0.4 to 0.8) was that boundary plane. The author's matching-radius check (the 18:52 UTC request) comes out cleanly **at equal budgets**: E(< R) agrees across the boxes to 0.2 to 0.6 percent out to R 21 for `S_1` and R 15 for `S_d`, so their interiors are box-independent and the box dependence is exterior (the far-field tail for `S_1`, the string for `S_d`); for `S_0` the two descents sit at different stages and no radius matches. Against the 9000-step n32 rows nothing matches at any R, which is the convergence state, not the box. So on this instrument the three totals are one intrinsic interior each plus a boundary-and-exterior part that differs by object. (iii) **The Derrick reads say why nothing converges, with a qualifier**: every relaxed object has a virial far above 3 and a negative `dE / d lambda`; for the compact `S_1` family (2 to 13 percent of the energy in the shell) the dilation derivative is within 1.2 to 1.7 of the Derrick value `-E_curv + 3 V` and `R_* = r (E_curv / 3 V)^(1/4)` is 13 to 18 in a box of half-width 24, so at `W1 = 7.2e-4` the potential is too weak to set the electron's size inside these boxes and the FALLING label is the dilation mode; for `S_d` and `S_0` the frozen shell carries 41 to 52 percent of that derivative (dilating moves the seed's boundary data), so their numbers read the boundary as much as the field; the virial falls with the budget (23 to 14.5 for `S_1` over the extension), so it reads the convergence state too. (iv) **Every undressed minimum is a saddle in the full sector** along the record's boost dressing (`d2E / ds2` from -31 to -1672), with both potentials exactly silent: the author's items 1 and 4 of 18:52 UTC measured on our own fields, R19's RUNAWAY rows and § 590 seen from the static side. (v) **The triple**: `AXES_DEGENERATE` by the pre-registered resolution rule (the largest increment, 22.9, exceeds a third of the smallest pairwise difference, 17.8), while the ordering `S_1 < S_0 < S_d` holds on every box and budget; the read-only Koide `Q` is 0.38 at n64 (ratios 1 : 2.36 : 6.38), 0.41 under `V_spec` at n32: nowhere near 2/3, and not a gate. (vi) **`V_spec` against `V4`**: under `V_spec` the descents converge to machine precision by moving the spatial spectrum off the target (the outer shells at (0.36, 0.38, 0.98) instead of (0, 0.3, 1), the transverse pair degenerate, the axis gap 0.001), the same fields costing 8 to 10 under `V4`; the electron keeps its winding at E 4.885, the `delta`-axis and `0`-axis objects unwind (`AXIS_ESCAPES`), which is what `pi_2 = 0` on the biaxial vacuum predicts and what the author's 16:30 UTC comment accepted in advance. (vii) **The controls**: the transverse swaps end within 1.5 percent of their partners after the extension (9.64 / 9.78, 61.86 / 60.88, 22.90 / 22.76: the transverse pair relaxes to the same state whichever eigenvalue the seed put on `phi`); on the degenerate vacuum the two `delta`-axis assignments end 4 percent apart (16.55 / 17.21; their frozen shells already differ by a third of that gap and both are FALLING, so the null is neither passed nor failed), the uniaxial electron `S_1^dd` at 3.99 against the biaxial 9.78 (the split and its line defect cost about 6 at this box), and the g 32 control reproduces R3's record (18.963 against 18.970 at 1500 steps, CONVERGED).

**What this settles for § 363 to 364 of the report.** On the certified static sector the three-axis mechanism does not produce three energies of one object family: all three keep their winding under `V4` (solid-angle degree 1), but the `delta`-axis object is a polar string (its energy grows linearly with the box, interior growth, a tension of 0.12 per unit length in the tube's flat middle), the `0`-axis object carries the same line defect with most of its cost in the boundary layer, and the electron is compact; under the author's `V_spec` two of the three unwind. The paper's protection lives on the uniaxial vacuum, where the trio collapses to one winding object (`S_1^dd`) and two textures without a winding eigenvector. None of the quoted energies is a converged intrinsic number (the Derrick and matching-radius reads), which is the report's own § 589.4 step 1 (existence first) answered in the static sector: the size is set by the box, not by the potential, at `W1`.

**The audit** ([`m5_32_r20_1_audit.py`](../scripts/m5_32_r20_1_audit.py), [`m5_32_r20_1_audit.json`](../data/m5_32_r20_1_audit.json), a fresh agent, 40 s, 3.7 GB): 50 own lines, 41 PASS, 9 FAIL, every FAIL a refutation of a reading and none of a number: the energies of all 25 fields to 2.7e-14 (own jets, own bracket, the potentials through the spectrum), the seed identity 0.0 at three configurations, the labels to 1e-12, the dilation slopes and virials, the boost curvatures to 1e-11, the tube reads and E(< R) tables to 1e-8, the two-rung outcomes and the Koide arithmetic identical; the refutations D1 (the string label supported for `S_d` only), D2 (the matching radius exists at equal budgets), D3 (the pinned shell carries half of the dilation derivative for `S_d` and `S_0`), D4 (`S_d` keeps its winding: solid-angle degree 1.000, the Mermin 0.6 under-resolved at the near-degenerate axis), D5 (the flux on degenerate ranks and the conflict counts are algorithm-dependent, not numbers), D6 (the tube tension quoted first is the boundary plane; the flat middle 0.12 / 0.06 / 0.01), D7 (the degenerate null undecided), D8 (the extension rows' decades are from the resumed field) are all folded into the reading above.

**Not in R20 (the cuts and the next rungs).** The n48 rung, the n64 extension and the degenerate-vacuum trio's ladder (cut on the run day for the review); a boundary that does not hold the seed's line defect (a pin at the relaxed far field, or a free boundary), which is what a fair three-axis comparison needs; the sigma term `tr(dM dM)` (the report's v12 of the 21:28 UTC record, the flat-vacuum quadratic that R20-0 (d) shows is zero) and the commutator-free quartics `I_2` to `I_5` (v13), both in the stack's registry since R0 and R15: the R21 candidates; items 2 and 3 of the author's 18:52 UTC comment (not in the posted body).

**What the plan check settles before the go** (algebra and reads on the R20 record in the terminal on 2026-09-14; R21-0 carries the parts that are computations as a script with an audit).

**The object, once.** The three axes of R20 (`S_1 (1, delta, 0)`, `S_d (delta, 1, 0)`, `S_0 (0, 1, delta)` on `4 I1 + V4`, g 8, delta 0.3, the same seeds, the same instrument) under three boundaries, compared at one residual:

```text
B_seed   the R20 boundary: the Dirichlet shell (depth 1.6) at the seed's values, the seed's line defect frozen at half-width 24
B_far    the Dirichlet shell at the relaxed far field: the R20 n64 end field's values on the n32 box's shell (the central 32^3 of the 64^3 field)
B_free   no pin: every cell relaxes, the faces one-sided (the natural boundary of the discrete functional)
```

Pre-registered against the report's reading. (i) The seed's line defect is intrinsic to the three-axis assignment (the frame `(n, phi, theta)` is singular on the polar axis for every assignment; the cost is the transverse eigenvalue carried by `phi` or `theta`, which is why `S_d` holds 72 percent of its energy in the shell and `S_1` 7 percent), so no pinned boundary can be "fair" to the three: the only boundary that does not hold the defect is the free one, and the relaxed-far-field pin is the intermediate that keeps the box's far field at a value a bigger box chose. (ii) With `pi_2 = 0` on the biaxial vacuum and every R20 object wanting to dilate, the free boundary can let the string escape through the faces or the electron fill the box; both are outcomes, not failures, and the reads below name them.

**R21-0, the form level (one script, `m5_32_r21_0_form.py`, audited).** (a) The free-boundary gate: `energy_grad` with the pin mask dropped equals the stack's call (it is the same call), and the gradient at face and edge cells passes a 4-point stencil at 1e-11 relative (the cells the pin zeroed since R3) and a complex step at 1e-13 on an n8 box. (b) The `B_far` seed: the coordinate coincidence to 1e-15, the restricted field's shell values equal to the n64 field's bitwise, the restricted field's energy on the n32 box and its shell energy against the seed's (45.5 for `S_d` under `B_seed`). (c) § 711.3's texture on the certified action: `M_s = M_vac + eps [E_00 f(x / s) + (E_01 + E_10) q(x / s)]` with the author's compact bumps, on the n32 lattice, `s` in {12, 9, 6, 4.5, 3} and `eps` in {0.5, 1, 2, 4}, the split `E_curv(s)` and `V4(s)`, the fit to `-a eps^4 / s + b^2 eps^2 s + c s^3`, at delta 0.3 (this stack's vacuum) and at the author's delta 1 vacuum `(-8, 1, 1, 0)`; the jet value `tr(F_0 eta F_0 eta)` reproduced (-2 stated) and the Frobenius pairing's +2 as a form-level control only (this stack has no Frobenius branch). (d) The two-roots prediction from the R20 record alone: the `(g - 1)^2` ratio above, written as the pre-registration of R21-3. Added on the author's reply (the plan-check numbers in [`m5_32_r21_plan_check.py`](../scripts/m5_32_r21_plan_check.py), its record [`m5_32_r21_plan_check.json`](../data/m5_32_r21_plan_check.json)): (e) the valley slope `nu(Delta) = argmin_nu V(nu + Delta, nu, nu, nu)`, `nu' = -V_nD / V_nn`, for V4 in exact arithmetic at `Delta = 0` and `Delta = -9`, with the separable control (-1/4), the rank-weighted control (`-a / (a + 3 b)` as the right-derivative at `Delta -> 0+`, where `lambda_max = nu + Delta`; -0.5 at a 3, b 1, reproduced; from the left the same weights give -1/6, the kink of a non-smooth symmetric V) and the symmetric-point identity `V_nD / V_nn = (A + 3 B) / (4 A + 12 B) = 1 / 4` for any permutation-symmetric V; (f) the norm along the boost on the R20 n32 end fields (the three axes under V4 and `V_spec`, the g 32 row): `tr(M^2)` pointwise at s 0.01 / 0.02 / 0.05 against the closed forms `4 tr(M^2 K)` and `8 tr(K^2 M^2) + 8 tr(K M K M)`, and the curvature `d2E / ds2` along the norm-preserving path (the boost followed by the per-cell rescale that restores `tr(M^2)(x)`) against R20's raw curvatures; (g) the escape-route calibration on the two R20 `V_spec` escapes (`S_d`, `S_0`): the pointwise spatial-block norm against 1.09 and the eigenvalue crossings along the former polar axis, so that the route vocabulary is calibrated on a known escape before R21-1 uses it.

**R21-1, the boundary triple on n32 (the run).** For each axis: `B_far` from the n64 restriction and `B_free` from the R20 9000-step n32 end field (the interior already relaxed, only the boundary changes), FIRE 4500 accepted steps, the R20 kill rules, the checkpoints every 500 steps, the pools detached and memory-budgeted (the R20 lessons). Reads per row as R20 (the degree per eigenvector on the r 6 / 9 / 12 cubes with the solid-angle degree, the shells' eigenvalues, the tube read with its flat middle, the shell energy, the Derrick reads, the frame reads, `E(< R)` on the shells with the matching radius across boundaries), plus three new reads: Mikulski's tail slope (the sign of the fitted slope of the shell density on r 8 to 16), the face-layer energy under `B_free` (the outer 1.6 of the box, the cells the pin used to hold), and the pointwise norm of the spatial block `tr(M_3x3^2)` against the vacuum's 1.09 (its profile along the former polar axis and on the r 6 / 9 / 12 shells, the escape-route read of the outcomes table). Optional, if the budget allows: `S_d` under `B_free` at n64 from the R20 n64 end field, 2000 steps (the string's escape at the larger box).

**R21-2, the polish (the equal-residual bar).** Every n32 row of the triple (the three R20 `B_seed` 9000-step rows included) polished by scipy L-BFGS (memory 20) on the free entries with the same `energy_grad`, the gate POLISHED iff `max |G| < 1e-3` (a decade under the best FIRE end of R20) within 3000 iterations, else the residual recorded and the row qualified "(at FALLING, `max |G|` = ...)"; the comparisons across boundaries use POLISHED rows, and where a row does not reach the gate the comparison is stated at the largest residual among the three rows of that axis, taken from the L-BFGS traces. The Mikulski bar (1e-4) is reported if reached, not required.

**R21-3, the (g, delta) scan of the frame saddle (the two-roots read).** `S_1` under `B_seed` at n32: g in {1, 2, 4, 16} at delta 0.3 (g 8 and g 32 are R20's rows: six g points, the g 16 row added on the author's reply because a pure `g^2` law and a `(g - 1)^2` law differ by 15 percent there and only by the measured spread at g 32) and delta in {0.6, 1.0} at g 8 (at delta 1 the transverse pair is degenerate, the R20 degenerate-trio seed builder covers it), 3000 accepted steps plus the R21-2 polish, then the R20 frame read (`d2E / ds2` along the R3 boost dressing at s 0.01, 0.02 and 0.05, extrapolated to `s -> 0`, both potentials' silence), and the free-root fits of the outcomes table. If the stack's configuration rejects g 1 exactly (the vacuum `diag(1, 1, 0.3, 0)`), the row runs at g 1.05 and says so.

**R21-4, the W1 ladder (the report's § 776 request, three rows).** `S_1` under `B_seed` at n32 with `W1` times 5 and times 25 (the `v4` potential hook with the same targets and the scaled weight; the seed and the pin unchanged), and `S_d` at `W1` times 25, 4500 accepted steps plus the R21-2 polish, the R20 reads with the Derrick reads first: `R_*`, the virial, `dE / d lambda`, `E(< R)` and the shell share. The ladder is under `B_seed` so that the rows compare with R20's; the `W1` times 25 `S_1` row is also run under `B_free` if the budget allows (the localized object should not care about the boundary).

**Amended on the author's 14:45 UTC reply ([discussioncomment-18435636](https://github.com/vantasnerdan/substrate-framework/discussions/186#discussioncomment-18435636)), the same day.** The reply confirms three plan-check numbers (the escape -48.0200 at (8, 0.3); `R_*` 5.8 to 8.0 at W1 times 25 against 6 to 8 here; the frame ratio 17 to 19 sitting between a pure `(g - 1)^2` law, 19.61, and a pure `g^2` law, 16.0, "exactly the situation your scan is designed to resolve"), accepts the two corrections on the author's side (§ 767's two-point fit recorded as underdetermined; § 765's lepton reading withdrawn as the sixty-first corrected claim, Koide's scale-invariant Q kept as the read, 0.3802 / 0.3984 on R20's axes; both in the record as § 849, revision 923, not on zenodo at this writing), states "no stake in any particular outcome of R21-1 or R21-3", and adds four inputs, each evidence until the checks run. | the Landau-de Gennes literature (the Lyuksyutov regime) predicts `STRING_ESCAPES`: the radial hedgehog unstable, the minimizers biaxial with the eigenvalues exchanging on the disclination lines; asked that the plan pre-register it as the expected outcome; the norm constraint `tr(M^2)` fixed, derivable from that regime, preferred to § 767's determinant | the record's own § 13.9 already carries the biaxial ring (Penzenstadler and Trebin 1989; Gartland and Mkaddem 2000; the radial hedgehog a transition state, Majumdar 2012) as R15's end state, with a threshold in `g W` for the core to acquire it; that regime is the potential-dominated one (the norm held at its vacuum value, the eigenvalues exchanging), and R20 at W1 measured the opposite side (the virial 14 to 80, the `V_spec` shells leaving the spectrum to (0.36, 0.38, 0.98)), so at W1 the literature's route is not the expected one and the R21-4 ladder is the walk toward it; the plan pre-registers the two escape routes (`ESCAPE_BY_EXCHANGE`, `ESCAPE_BY_MELTING`, calibrated in R21-0 (g)), and `STRING_ESCAPES` reads the same either way, as asked |
| the norm and the determinant "both have exactly zero overlap" with the boost direction | refined by the plan check: the boost conjugates `N = M eta` (`Q^T eta Q = eta`), so every eigenvalue function of N (V4's `tr N^p`, `det N`) and `det M` (`det Q = 1`) are silent to all orders (the spectrum drift 3.6e-15, R20's item 6 measured 1e-13), while the Euclidean norm `tr(M^2)` is silent at first order on block-diagonal fields (R20's static rows, `M_0i = 0`; sufficient, not necessary: `4 tr(M^2 K) = 8 (M_00 M_01 + M_01 M_11 + M_02 M_12 + M_03 M_13)`, 43.8 on a general symmetric M) and grows at second order (648 at the vacuum, 575 on a block-diagonal sample): a pointwise norm constraint is not a level set of the escape, and whether it lifts or lowers the floor is R21-0 (f), pre-registered `NORM_SILENT` / `NORM_LIFTS` / `NORM_LOWERS` |
| the light-trajectory slope `nu' = -1/4` on `(nu + Delta, nu, nu, nu)` for every separable `V = sum f(lambda_i)`, evaded only by rank weighting (`-a / (a + 3 b)`); "if V4 is a symmetric function of the eigenvalues, this may constrain what the W1 ladder can show" | V4 is symmetric in the eigenvalues of N and not separable (a squared power sum), and gives `nu' = -1/4` at `Delta = 0` all the same: the reason is permutation symmetry alone (at a symmetric point `V_nn = 4 A + 12 B`, `V_nD = A + 3 B` for any symmetric V, the ratio 1/4 exact), so the theorem is broader than the separable class and rank weighting evades it exactly because it breaks the symmetry; at the hierarchy, `Delta = -9` (the light triple near 1), V4's valley slope is -1.005 on the near-vacuum branch (+0.049 on a second branch at nu 5.99), where a quadratic `sum (lambda_i - Lambda_i)^2` keeps -1/4 at every Delta because `f''` is constant: at the physical spectrum the form is the variable (§ 790's `kappa_V` from -1/4 is a `Delta = 0` number on that potential); R21-0 (e) carries the audited number; nothing in R21-4 changes, the W1 ladder being a scale test (the overall factor), not a form test |
| four one-claim verification files offered (`claim_848_universal.py`, `claim_850_ordering.py`, `claim_814_blind.py`, `claim_763_inc2G.py`) | accepted in the reply: run unchanged under this stack's environment, filed under `theory/` (gitignored), their results in the R21 code map's validation column as the cross-model step |

The plan check was audited the same day by a fresh agent with its own methods (the full 4x4 Hessian in eigenvalue space on four potentials, one random in the power sums; the valley re-minimized at `Delta +- h` on a matrix evaluation of V4; sympy's matrix exponential on a generic symmetric M and 20 random boosts): 27 checks, none failed ([`m5_32_r21_plan_check_audit.py`](../scripts/m5_32_r21_plan_check_audit.py)); its two qualifications are in the text above (the rank-weighted slope is one-sided at `Delta = 0`; block-diagonal is sufficient, not necessary, for the first-order silence), and its note that `W1` cancels in every `nu'` (a ratio) means the valley slope tests the form of V4 and nothing about its weight.

**Budget and cuts.** R21-0 under one hour. R21-1 six n32 rows at 4500 steps (about 1.3 h each, six in parallel at 0.7 GB), R21-3 six rows at 3000 steps and R21-4 three rows at 4500 steps, together under 12 workers and the 12 GB memory budget (15 n32 rows at 0.7 GB, two waves), about 3 h wall; R21-2 nine to eighteen polishes at up to 3000 L-BFGS iterations (about 1 s each at n32), about 1 h wall in parallel; the optional n64 string row 2000 steps, about 3.5 h at 3.5 GB, started with the pools and cut without penalty if it is not in at collect; one fresh-agent audit per stage; the review about 8 h after the go. Cut first if the day runs long: the n64 row, then the `B_free` copy of the `W1` times 25 row, then the delta scan of R21-3 (the g 16 row stays: it is the row that separates the two laws).

**Not in R21 (the registry).** The sigma term `tr(dM dM)` (the report's v12; § 661's one term that lifts every one-parameter texture, report 029 chs. 134 to 139's released cylinder core with its `2 pi sigma ln R` line energy): the R22 candidate, a new energy branch with its own R0-style certification; v23 (gauged SO(3) to U(1)) and v24 (Hermitian M): new field content, not this stack; `inc(h) = 2 G(h)`, the `J != 0` upper-bound reading and Newton by the sourced constraint (§ 718: supplied, not derived): nothing to run here; the degenerate trio's ladder and the n48 rung (still cut); a per-box W1 (Mikulski's per-box gamma, likewise not run).

### 6.12 R21 run: at W1 the certified potential holds neither the seed's line defect nor the vacuum spectrum, the three energies were the pin's, R20's g 32 "CONVERGED" row polishes from 18.96 to 6.60, the frame saddle is a `g^2` law above g 8 with no root at delta 1, and the W1 x 25 electron is compact (RUN 2026-09-14, the go 16:30 UTC; the record in [`m5_32_task_details.md`](../tasks/m5_32_task_details.md), the R21 section)

**The instrument.** [`m5_32_r21_1_runs.py`](../scripts/m5_32_r21_1_runs.py) on the R20 instrument consumed read-only: the same energy, seeds and reads, the R20 FIRE copied with the pin optional, and the R21-2 polish inside every job (scipy L-BFGS, memory 20, on the ten upper-triangle entries per free cell, the gate `max |G| < 1e-3` on the free cells within 3000 iterations). The bar was reached on three rows only (g 1, 2, 4); every other row ends FALLING at `max |G|` 0.02 to 7.9 after 3000 iterations, and the residual travels with every number below; the polish lowered E on every row but raised `max |G|` on ten of them (L-BFGS minimizes E, not the max-norm), so the boundary triples sit at residuals that differ by factors 1.7 to 12 within an axis: not one residual, the audit's wording correction. The polish moved every R20 row by more than the boundary did: the three R20 `B_seed` end fields (9000 FIRE steps) went 9.78 to 5.68 (`S_1`), 60.88 to 55.13 (`S_d`), 22.76 to 15.52 (`S_0`), and R20's g 32 row (CONVERGED by the R20 gate, R3's 18.97) went 18.96 to 6.60 with its residual still 1.0: R20's convergence gate (the last-third drift and two decades of `max |G|`) read a FIRE stall, and every R3 to R20 energy on this stack at W1 is a stalled number. The polished fields pass the integrity reads (`M_0i` exactly 0, the pinned shells bitwise, the traces monotone; the audit's checks below).

What the triple says: R20's three energies 9.8 / 60.9 / 22.8 were the pinned shell's (the seed's frozen line defect: 46.0 of `S_d`'s 55.1 and 11.5 of `S_0`'s 15.5 sit in the shell after the polish, against 0.85 for `S_1`), and once the shell is released the three axes fall to 1.3 to 4.2 on fields that fill the box (`r_half` 16 to 18 of the half-width 24, `R_*` 14 to 18), where the dilation scan (expand) and the Derrick identity `-E_curv + 3 V` (contract: the virial 1.6 to 2.8 is below 3) disagree in sign; the audit's split shows the interior, not the faces, carrying 82 to 97 percent of the scan's negative slope, so the two measure different things on a field that is nowhere at the vacuum, the box read is undecided, and R20's `AXIS_BOX_LIMITED` does not carry over to the polished free rows. The triple's `AXES_DEGENERATE` is forced by the resolution's construction (three times `S_d`'s seed-minus-free gap, the frozen shell), whatever the free energies are. The relaxed far field (`B_far`) carries 11 to 20 times less shell energy than the seed pin and gives the same interior as `B_seed` to 5 percent for `S_1` and 10 percent for `S_d` (E(< 12)), so the interior is boundary-independent between the two pins while the totals are the shell's. **`FRAME_NOT_PRODUCT`** (the free-root fits: `g_0` = -0.94 with a 33 percent miss, `delta_0` = 2.9, the product missing 39 percent), with two readings the numbers support and one caveat. Above g 8 the law is `g^2`: 685 / 42.7 = 16.05 against 16.0 for `g^2` and 19.6 for `(g - 1)^2`, and 166 / 42.7 = 3.90 against 4.0 and 4.59 (the g 16 row, added on the author's reply, decides it at 2.5 percent; the audit's fit on the three FALLING rows alone gives `g_0` -0.015 to 1.8 percent, and on the three POLISHED, unwound rows `(g + 0.84)^2` to 0.1 percent): the pure `g^2` alternative the author named, not the `(g - 1)^2` factor of § 591 / § 767, on the rows that keep their winding. There is no root near delta 1: the curvature at delta 1 is -21.8, half the delta 0.3 value, on a row where the transverse pair is degenerate by construction (the escape `-2 (delta - 1)^2 (g - 1)^2` predicts zero there). The caveat: at g 1, 2 and 4 the electron unwinds (the winding degree 0 on the r 9 cube with the spectrum at the vacuum, the winding pushed into the pinned shell), so the low-g rows measure a different object and the two-root question is answered on g 8 to 32 only; and the g 8 curvature moved from -87.5 (R20's 9000-step field) to -42.7 after the polish, so R20's ratio 19.1 was a residual artifact.

**R21-0, the form level** (the seven parts and their audit are in the task record; the outcomes): (a) the free boundary is the same functional (the face-cell gradient to 1.9e-11); (b) the `B_far` seed exact; (c) § 711.3's texture on `4 I1 + V4`: **`TEXTURE_UNBOUNDED`** in the curvature term at both vacua (`E_curv < 0`, exactly quartic in eps, the `-1 / s` law within 10 percent above the lattice floor) with `f = q` or any two radial profiles giving `F = 0` in the continuum (the texture needs independent gradients), while `V4 = c s^3` swamps it at every lattice s (the crossing scale 0.14 to 0.37 below h at W1); (d) the pre-registration; (e) the valley slope: `V4` -1.005 and `V_spec` -0.9997 at the hierarchy, both the stiff-heavy limit `-1 / (1 + 3 f''(1) / f''(-8))` (-0.999667 on R20-0's `V_spec` curvatures), the author's `kappa_V` target -0.807 needing a light-to-heavy curvature ratio of 0.08 that neither degree-8 potential has; (f) **`NORM_SILENT`** at `s -> 0` on all seven R20 fields: the norm restoration is a quartic wall `k s^4` with dips of 0.001 to 0.005 under `V4` (the plan check's `NORM_LOWERS` wrong, and the s 0.02 labels step artifacts); (g) the `V_spec` escapes are **`ESCAPE_BY_MELTING`** (`V_spec` is free at the isotropic zero), and the norm read names a route only where the degree says the winding is lost.

**Against the readings on record.** The record's § 765 (three energies in the right order) and § 849's Koide read: the order `S_1 < S_0 < S_d` survives the pin's release (4.17 / 1.73 / 1.27 reverses it: `S_d < S_0 < S_1` under `B_free`), and the three are degenerate at the boundary resolution, as the author's own "every energy in both reports is an artefact of where the boundary was placed" said; the record's 18.97 single at g 32 (R3, the seed of every pair law since) is a FIRE-stalled number, 6.60 after the polish with the spectrum drifted, a correction on our side of the same kind as R20's convergence labels. The Lyuksyutov expectation (`STRING_ESCAPES`) is met, and by its own route, which the plan said would not be reached at W1: the tension goes with the pin, and the rank-1 eigenvector of `S_d` is non-orientable on the r 9 surface on every boundary (a defect line pierces the cube: the non-orientability read of the radial hedgehog turned disclination texture, the Penzenstadler and Trebin end state of the record's own § 13.9, its biaxiality not read here), while `S_0`'s winding is lost outright on every boundary at the vacuum norm (exchange by the norm rule under the seed pin). The norm constraint does not close the boost escape at second order (R21-0 (f)). The two-roots structure of § 591 / § 767 is not on this stack: `g^2` above g 8, no delta root.

**What the rung says to the author.** (1) The small-delta constant 0.258 is the stack's number for the smooth-core strand. (2) Under the certified action that strand is a saddle: textures with pi-walls of the pair anisotropy and unmelted cores sit below the Bogomolny value at every spacing tried and re-form on refinement; the action as written has no tension floor for a half-line, and the free-charge bound from `L_* = m / sigma` inherits that; whether a smoothness term is the intended repair is the author's call. (3) The physical rotation generator does not make the charge's inertia box-independent on the relaxed charge, because the relaxed charge is not axisymmetric (the split half-lines, the director off radial inside r 9). (4) The census: three certified branches whose energies differ at the percent level, with the boundary field at the same level; a free-shell twin is the instrument for a branch count. (5) The catalog fault was known since M5.21.3; R25's sentence "every kin_of read since M5.21.3 is suspect" is withdrawn: the affected readers are the two AFFECTED and nine MIXED scripts of the census, the R12 to R15 and R10 instruments built their own tangents.
